# Skills · U · part 2

841 entries, uefn-knowledge to ui-first-principles. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **uefn-knowledge**: The UEFN/Fortnite knowledge base behind uefn-ai-toolkit — validated asset and device class paths (with the confirmed-invalid ones), how-to recipes for scripting devices and Verse, and non-obvious unreal.* gotchas that silently corrupt work. Use before spawning or validating any Fortnite asset, before concluding a device setting or item content can't be scripted, before any bulk transform work, before wiring devices together, when a spawn/move/save appears to succeed but doesn't persist, or whenever a class path or asset needs to be discovered against a live editor. ([dylannalex/uefn-ai-toolkit](https://github.com/dylannalex/uefn-ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/uefn-knowledge) · ★1)
- **uefn-lessons**: Shared cross-project knowledge base of recurring Verse/UEFN gotchas, MCP quirks, and coding mistakes, accumulated across every project set up with this kit. Read it before writing or debugging Verse code, or when investigating a compile/runtime error, to check whether it's already a known issue. Also the place to add a new entry when you discover something non-obvious that would help on future projects too, not just this one. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-lessons) · ★1)
- **uefn-level-devices**: Place/move actors, tags, folders, components, data layers, classic devices, event bindings and entities in the UEFN level via MCP (SceneTools, ActorTools, DeviceToolset, EntityToolset). Use for level changes in the lab, always with final cleanup. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-level-devices) · ★1)
- **uefn-materials**: Create or edit a Material, Material Instance or parameter in the lab and apply it to an actor via the UEFN MCP (39/39 tools ok), and tidy a messy material graph (layout pass, no change in behavior). Use for materials work; only `verified` recipes, rest listed as not verified. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-materials) · ★1)
- **uefn-mcp-heavy-calls**: Cost limits of UEFN MCP reads: which calls return huge payloads and how to filter them. Use before any potentially large read. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-mcp-heavy-calls) · ★1)
- **uefn-mcp-lab-guard**: UEFN MCP lab guard: path forms (GUID vs LAB_ROOT), shell, what the guard refuses. Use before any MCP tool that WRITES, or any script that sets LAB_ROOT/LAB_AGGRESSIVE. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-mcp-lab-guard) · ★1)
- **uefn-pcg**: Scatters props, trees and rocks over an area of a UEFN map with a PCG graph made through the MCP, instead of placing each one by hand. Use when the owner asks for a forest, a field of rocks, many props, scenery, scatter, procedural placement, or a map area that would take hours to dress by hand. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-pcg) · ★1)
- **uefn-physics-mesh**: Read mesh, skeletal mesh, physics asset and texture data and add primitives via the UEFN MCP (50/63 tools ok, read-only skill). Use for mesh/physics reads; only `verified` recipes, rest listed as not verified. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-physics-mesh) · ★1)
- **uefn-profiling**: Measures a UEFN map's performance through the MCP (Spatial Profiler metrics and hotspots, Insights trace captures and memory snapshots) and turns the numbers into a short list of fixes. Use when the owner says lag, fps, hitch, performance, memory, profile, budget, hotspot, or before a publish that must stay under budget. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-profiling) · ★1)
- **uefn-quests**: Builds custom quests with the Verse Progression API (quest, objective, rewards, collection, participant) that UEFN 43.00 lets you publish. Use when the owner asks for a quest, mission, objective chain, collect-N task, tracked goal, or a reward for finishing something. ([mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team](https://github.com/mimmo-the-root/UEFN-dream-bot-team/tree/HEAD/user-level-skills/uefn-quests) · ★1)
- **uefn-scripting-batch**: Run 5+ MCP calls server-side in one request with ProgrammaticToolset (get_execution_environment, execute_tool_script) for batched reads, widget chains and edit+build. Use when several calls can share one request and cleanup is planned. Requires uefn-mcp-lab-guard. Use for session health, always-close and regression verdicts. Requires uefn-mcp-lab-guard and uefn-verse-build. - **uexecute**: Use to implement an approved plan. When the plan declares `### Interface graph`, derives waves by topo-sort over `[blocks]` IF edges only — non-blocking IF edges (the default) put producer and consumer in the same wave, since the IF declaration is sufficient context. Edits inline when only one implementer would fire (single phase or serial fallback). Runs plan-diff check and consistency sweep after each commit. Forbids silent fallbacks and mutation of external spec/design docs. Dispatches the planner skill when deviations invalidate the plan. ([btseytlin/ultrapack](https://github.com/btseytlin/ultrapack/tree/HEAD/skills/uexecute) · ★47)
